Plenium Builders is a general contractor serving clients and working together with design partners to create high-quality commercial buildings and unique custom homes.

# Culture

At Plenium Builders, we're driven by family, teamwork, and a deep sense of community. Our foundation rests on traditions that have been a part of the fabric of our region for over 60 years. The bonds we've forged are clear and strong, with some of us having worked together for over two decades. Our team has transformed many dreams into tangible realities and crafted cherished community spaces.

We believe the heart of a company beats loudest through its people. Team members spearhead our Good Works Committee and Team-Building Committee, set up to direct corporate giving and volunteering and employee events, respectively. From supporting the community to celebrating our shared milestones, these groups ensure we're always connected and engaged, both with each other and the community around us.

Choosing Plenium Builders means more than just hiring a contractor; it means becoming part of our extended family. A family that treasures shared knowledge, mutual support, and the joy of making a meaningful difference. As we continue our journey, we're committed to building on our values, nurturing relationships, and enhancing our legacy every step of the way.

## MISSION

To be your first-choice builder, set the industry standard, and grow the community together.

## VISION

Your premier general contractor in a vibrant Sierra Nevada market.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/d5c2388d-0cb9-4be4-a40e-be0ddd0beff3/logo-pattern-01.jpg)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/8242192b-e296-4535-845d-a374d76ded55/value-01-skill-plenium-builders-rev.png)

## OUR VALUES

### Skill

Skill represents our pursuit of excellence, continuous growth, and mastery of our craft. We pursue a flawless result for every project.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/6ba056ae-9a0c-4553-a0ef-e3f93d92a26c/value-02-heart-plenium-builders-rev.png)

### Heart

Heart embodies our passion, commitment, and sincerity. We are dedicated to our clients, teams, and each other.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/e31415b3-413a-4930-88cf-d4d46866e3b9/value-03-community-plenium-builders-rev.png)

### Community

Community reflects our commitment to collaboration and mutual success. We have a positive impact on the places we serve.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/fe3f4218-6a76-4a75-a333-d2560073b708/pb+good+works+committee+logo.png)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/63f7f7e3-76b0-40cf-93e0-138965815d18/culture-giftwrap-2024.jpg)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/33c31393-6480-48e3-b46a-23cf89fb3f47/culture-reno-rescue-2024.jpg)

## Committed to Community

**Real impact starts from within. Our people decide where and how we give.**

The Good Works Committee is a team of employees who lead our charitable giving and community involvement. They decide where we donate, which causes we support, and how we show up for the communities where we live and build. It’s not a corporate formality—it’s a reflection of our culture of trust, inclusion, and shared purpose.

Through thoughtful collaboration, the committee directs funding, coordinates volunteer efforts, and strengthens our relationships with organizations doing meaningful work.

Here are just a few of the groups we've been proud to support with volunteer activities, collection drives, event participation, and financial support:

-   [Boys and Girls Club of Truckee Meadows](https://bgctm.org/)
    
-   [Eddy House](https://eddyhouse.org/)
    
-   [The Salvation Army Reno Corps](https://reno.salvationarmy.org/)
    
-   [The Discovery](https://nvdm.org/)
    
-   [Society of Women Engineers Sierra Nevada Section](https://sns.swe.org/)
    
-   [Food Bank of Northern Nevada](https://fbnn.org/)
    

-   Teal Fleur De Lis Foundation
    
-   [Renown Children’s Hospital](https://www.renown.org/)
    
-   [Nevada Recreation & Park Society](https://nevadarecreationampparksociety.wildapricot.org/)
    
-   [The Children’s Cabinet](https://www.childrenscabinet.org/)
    
-   [Reno Rescue Equine Therapy](https://www.renorescue.org/)
    
-   [Junior Achievement of Northern Nevada](https://northernnevada.ja.org/)
    
-   [MATHCOUNTS Northern Nevada Competitions](https://www.mathcounts.org/)

# CAREERS

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/d5c2388d-0cb9-4be4-a40e-be0ddd0beff3/logo-pattern-01.jpg)

## Build a career at  
Plenium Builders

We love teamwork, hard work, and tangible results. We love challenges, details, expertise, technology, and learning new things. We want to win, be the best, do stellar work, and please our clients. We enjoy helping others. We like one another and have fun together.

## Why Plenium Builders?

#### LOCATION

Northern Nevada offers a unique blend of big-city amenities and small-town charm, set against the stunning backdrop of the Sierra Nevada mountains. Its lower cost of living, less congested roadways, and proximity to outdoor recreation make the Reno-Tahoe region a desirable place to live and work.

#### Company Reputation and Brand

Become a part of a top-tier firm that sets standards for client service and building excellence. Plenium Builders’ ethical approach to business leadership in the community means we can be proud of what we accomplish every day.

#### BENEFITS and compensation

We offer a 401(k) plan with matching contributions and immediate vesting, health plan options with medical, dental, and vision coverages, and paid time off (PTO) with increases at major anniversaries.

#### Professional Development

Your growth is catalyzed by continuous training and diverse learning experiences. Stay updated and versatile, with professionally managed and tailored learning opportunities.

Hear it from Team Members

-   ## Devin Harrill
    
    ESTIMATOR
    
    “Working at Plenium Builders is really defined by the company culture, effective leadership, and ongoing professional development opportunities. The culture creates a fulfilling work environment. I’m happy to go to work every day.”
    
-   ## Taylor McGuire
    
    SENIOR ACCOUNTANT
    
    “Plenium Builders offers a great work/life balance, without sacrificing the quality work. The team that has been built here is phenomenal and the leaders set a great example for work ethic. I really enjoy being part of such an amazing environment.”
    
-   ## Mitch Medici
    
    SUPERINTENDENT
    
    “It feels like you’re going home when you show up for work whether it’s at the office or at a project. You have amazing team members that support you at each step on the project with vast industry experience to ensure a successful delivery.”
    
-   ## Lisa Frisch
    
    MARKETING DIRECTOR
    
    “I am surrounded by bright, dedicated, helpful, professional, and friendly people that inspire me and my best work. They are all terribly busy, but laughter regularly erupts from some room, office, or corner of the building. I’ve been incredibly lucky over the last 15 years in the Reno-Sparks area to work with some real superstars!”
    

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/e40441fe-1bd5-451e-96f4-1b9bbcc57ae6/premier-reno-tahoe-custom-homes-builder.jpg)

## WATCH IT IN ACTION

Our 2024 video celebration of the hard work, dedication, and skill that have marked our journey over the past year - set to music! It's a showcase of the diverse projects we are building or that we completed this year. Join us as we reflect on a year of building dreams into reality, one project at a time.

If you would like to join the Plenium Builders team, we encourage you to apply! To expedite your application, please look for our [current job postings on Indeed](https://www.indeed.com/cmp/Plenium-Builders). You may also email us your resume and cover letter at the address in the footer.

## How to Apply

# Services

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/d5c2388d-0cb9-4be4-a40e-be0ddd0beff3/logo-pattern-01.jpg)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/d0abe18e-0735-4af9-96a3-ad6f686faa49/villah-harrah-remodel-lake-tahoe01.jpg)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/9a1b76b5-1e76-464a-aa04-902ee5826a72/new-deantronics-nevada-23.jpg)

## Preconstruction Services

Whether you are creating a custom home or designing a new office building, Plenium Builders has the most effective planning and pre-construction tools to guide your project through all stages of design and construction. Think of this as a menu that can be customized around the particulars of your project. Let us help you achieve your goals.

-   #### Conceptual Estimate
    
    Plenium Builders prepares a detailed conceptual estimate from basic information like square footage, number of stories, systems, finish types and building site characteristics. This valuable tool gives you the power to prioritize where your budget will have the most impact to best accomplish your design goals.
    
-   #### Value Engineering
    
    Our team of experienced builders and estimators work together with designers to seek out alternative ways to achieve a similar function or aesthetic at a lower cost. We also suggest ways to reduce life cycle and operational costs with alternative materials or equipment.
    
-   #### Sustainable Building
    
    During preconstruction, Plenium Builders will help you plan for your sustainability goals by providing input on system strategies that reduce future operating costs and utilize renewable resources. We have knowledgeable experts and LEED® accredited professionals on hand to work with you during construction to achieve green project certification.
    

-   #### Virtual Building
    
    Our team can use your design team’s building information model (BIM) for quantity surveys, scheduling, and “clash” detection. These techniques reduce estimating time, increase coordination accuracy, and significantly mitigate cost and schedule risks.
    
-   #### Permit Support
    
    Our relationships and reputation with jurisdictional authorities – including local city and county planning staff members, utility agency representatives, environmental review boards and more – help your entire project team to build consensus for innovative construction strategies, develop plan-review phasing, and facilitate environmental approvals, permitting, inspections and sign-offs.
    
-   #### Schedule Planning
    
    Plenium Builders uses critical-path-method (CPM) schedule techniques and the latest, industry-leading software, Primavera, to effectively plan and lead your project. Our approach to project management is schedule-driven and focused on fulfilling our commitments on a granular level to move you in on time.
    

-   #### Constructability
    
    Our team of experienced builders and estimators work together with designers to seek out alternative ways to achieve a similar function or aesthetic at a lower cost. We also suggest ways to reduce life cycle and operational costs with alternative materials or equipment.
    
-   #### Life-Cycle Analysis
    
    We can provide life-cycle cost analysis to analyze long-term operational budget impacts vs. initial capital investments. Whether you want to analyze the whole project or just certain aspects, like major equipment, systems or materials, Plenium Builders’ estimating team can evaluate the best option to align with your budget priorities.
    
-   #### Budget Management
    
    As design progresses, you get regular estimate updates in parallel with your designer’s progress milestones. This includes variance reports and one-on-one support to walk you through the details. You will be well informed about where your budget dollars are being applied on your project.

J Resort’s reimagined 4th Street entrance improves arrival, visibility, and connection to downtown Reno. Completed within an active resort environment, the project supports ongoing investment in the Neon Line District and enhances the guest experience along one of the city’s most active hospitality corridors.

# [Renown Mail-Order Pharmacy Tenant Improvement](/portfolio-commercial/renown-mail-order-pharmacy)

This second-floor tenant improvement converted existing space in an operating support-services building into a dedicated mail-order pharmacy environment with offices, staff areas, casework, glazing, MEP upgrades, ceilings, lighting, and finishes. PB coordinated construction around ongoing operations while supporting infrastructure needs for specialized pharmacy automation equipment installed by others.

[Read More](/portfolio-commercial/renown-mail-order-pharmacy)

A vacant behavioral health facility in Reno is being renewed into a public behavioral health center serving Northern Nevada. Early CM-at-Risk collaboration, BIM-driven planning, and comprehensive systems upgrades support adaptive reuse, expanding care capacity while delivering modern, resilient healthcare infrastructure through coordinated construction.

# [Renown NW Reno Urgent Care Clinic](/portfolio-commercial/renown-nw-reno-urgent-care)

A former retail space was converted into an urgent care clinic with exam rooms, a lab, and medical communication systems. Phased delivery allowed Renown to begin operations ahead of final X-ray equipment installation, overcoming lead time delays while maintaining healthcare performance and patient care standards.

# Custom Homes

## BUILD YOUR  
Secret Lair  
dream Home  
Dream KITCHEN

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/792dca65-7971-4680-962d-3d63a8620512/premier-reno-tahoe-custom-homes-gc.jpg)

## with Plenium Builders

Featured

[Experience the Pinnacle of Construction Quality](/working-with-custom-homes/quality)

In the realm of custom homes and remodels, compromise is not an option. Our team takes on a select number of unique projects each year. This allows us to deliver superior construction quality with exceptional communication and a keen eye for detail. Every corner or curve of your project will be meticulously executed. We construct stunning and timeless homes and remodels.

[Read more →](/working-with-custom-homes/quality)

[Control Costs without Compromising Your Vision](/working-with-custom-homes/cost-control)

Keeping your budget in check does not have to mean forgoing the features you've dreamed of. We excel in estimating and delivering projects that combine luxury and affordability without sacrificing the quality of design or construction. Building your dream home, kitchen, or remodel is an investment, and we're here to make sure it is a cost-effective one.

[Read more →](/working-with-custom-homes/cost-control)

[Stay on Track with Our Efficient Building Process](/working-with-custom-homes/schedule)

Your time is as valuable as your investment. We recognize the importance of delivering projects on time without compromising on quality or shortcutting the collaboration with your designer. Our streamlined processes and experienced team work harmoniously to ensure we meet the projected completion dates. With us, building your dream home doesn’t have to mean endless waiting.

[Read more →](/working-with-custom-homes/schedule)

[Feel At Home with Sustainability](/working-with-custom-homes/sustainability)

Building or remodeling a custom home can be eco-friendly. With our knowledge of sustainable design elements and energy-efficient technologies, we can help you incorporate environmentally friendly features into your project. From solar panels to high-efficiency appliances, we can create a project that is as kind to the environment as it is luxurious and comfortable for you.

[Read more →](/working-with-custom-homes/sustainability)

## Custom Homes Team

Your home is more than just a place—it’s a reflection of your vision, lifestyle, and aspirations. At Plenium Builders, we understand the importance of creating a space that feels uniquely yours. Our custom homes team works closely with you and your design partners to bring that vision to life, combining innovative construction techniques with unmatched attention to detail. Backed by a fully background-checked workforce of skilled professionals, we ensure your home is built with the highest standards of quality, security, and care. From concept to completion, we deliver homes that are as extraordinary as the people who live in them.

## Custom Homes Portfolio

Featured

[

](/custom-homes-portfolio/hearthline-house)

[Hearthline House](/custom-homes-portfolio/hearthline-house)

A new custom home defined by structural timber, concealed details, and complex sitework. A continuous reveal ties the architecture together across finishes and materials. With evolving design input and flush detailing throughout, Plenium’s skilled carpenters executed framing and finish with precision—delivering seamless results.

[Read more →](/custom-homes-portfolio/hearthline-house)

[

](/custom-homes-portfolio/sagewinds-residence)

[Sagewinds Residence](/custom-homes-portfolio/sagewinds-residence)

This in-progress custom home includes insulated concrete walls, radiant heating, and slab oak cabinetry. The 3,900-SF residence also features structural steel, solar prep, and conduit-based wiring. Sitework began in May with clearing, trenching, and septic tank installation. The project is being built on a two-year timeline.

[Read more →](/custom-homes-portfolio/sagewinds-residence)

[

](/custom-homes-portfolio/brightside-ranch)

[Brightside Ranch](/custom-homes-portfolio/brightside-ranch)

Brightside Ranch was transformed with a reconfigured layout, structural upgrades, and a full exterior refresh. The team vaulted bedroom ceilings, brought two former garage areas up to code, and added crisp siding, limewashed brick, and a custom portico. The remodel improved flow, appearance, and livability throughout the home.

[Read more →](/custom-homes-portfolio/brightside-ranch)

[

](/custom-homes-portfolio/rubicon-bay)

[Rubicon Bay Residence](/custom-homes-portfolio/rubicon-bay)

This Lake Tahoe remodel included custom carpentry, full-scope interior upgrades, and the winter installation of three large marble slabs craned to the third floor. Features include a bunk room for eight with built-ins, new paneling and wallpaper throughout, a ski lift chair on the deck, and custom pieces crafted to fit each space.

[Read more →](/custom-homes-portfolio/rubicon-bay)

-   ## Holly Hollenbeck<span class="attribution">Principal Designer<br>HSH Interiors
    
    They jumped into a project with a demanding timeline and were clear with my firm and the clients about what could get done and by when. I recommend Plenium Builders to my fellow designers or architects as well as to homeowners.

# Industry Awards

Recognition for Quality, Craft, and Collaboration

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/6d7663d9-7415-42ce-b046-2d793b323f9b/blue+award.png)

We put care, craft, and pride into everything we build, so when our work gets recognized, we celebrate it. These awards reflect not just the end result, but the people, values, and teamwork that make it possible.

While recognition isn’t the reason we do what we do, it’s one of the ways we honor the effort behind the scenes and shine a light on the culture that drives it all.

Here are a few of the accolades that we have earned alongside our clients, design partners, subcontractors, and vendors:

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/a6d38171-933b-4124-b7e1-448051d6ae3c/plenium+builders+is+a+fun+place+to+work.jpg)

# Culture

At Plenium Builders, we're driven by family, teamwork, and a deep sense of community. Our foundation rests on traditions that have been a part of the fabric of our region for over 60 years. The bonds we've forged are clear and strong, with some of us having worked together for over two decades. Our team has transformed many dreams into tangible realities and crafted cherished community spaces.

We believe the heart of a company beats loudest through its people. Team members spearhead our Good Works Committee and Team-Building Committee, set up to direct corporate giving and volunteering and employee events, respectively. From supporting the community to celebrating our shared milestones, these groups ensure we're always connected and engaged, both with each other and the community around us.

Choosing Plenium Builders means more than just hiring a contractor; it means becoming part of our extended family. A family that treasures shared knowledge, mutual support, and the joy of making a meaningful difference. As we continue our journey, we're committed to building on our values, nurturing relationships, and enhancing our legacy every step of the way.

[View fullsize](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/33c31393-6480-48e3-b46a-23cf89fb3f47/culture-reno-rescue-2024.jpg)

## MISSION

To be your first-choice builder, set the industry standard, and grow the community together.

## VISION

Your premier general contractor in a vibrant Sierra Nevada market.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/d5c2388d-0cb9-4be4-a40e-be0ddd0beff3/logo-pattern-01.jpg)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/8242192b-e296-4535-845d-a374d76ded55/value-01-skill-plenium-builders-rev.png)

## OUR VALUES

### Skill

Skill represents our pursuit of excellence, continuous growth, and mastery of our craft. We pursue a flawless result for every project.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/6ba056ae-9a0c-4553-a0ef-e3f93d92a26c/value-02-heart-plenium-builders-rev.png)

### Heart

Heart embodies our passion, commitment, and sincerity. We are dedicated to our clients, teams, and each other.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/e31415b3-413a-4930-88cf-d4d46866e3b9/value-03-community-plenium-builders-rev.png)

### Community

Community reflects our commitment to collaboration and mutual success. We have a positive impact on the places we serve.

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/fe3f4218-6a76-4a75-a333-d2560073b708/pb+good+works+committee+logo.png)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/63f7f7e3-76b0-40cf-93e0-138965815d18/culture-giftwrap-2024.jpg)

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/33c31393-6480-48e3-b46a-23cf89fb3f47/culture-reno-rescue-2024.jpg)

## Committed to Community

**Real impact starts from within. Our people decide where and how we give.**

The Good Works Committee is a team of employees who lead our charitable giving and community involvement. They decide where we donate, which causes we support, and how we show up for the communities where we live and build. It’s not a corporate formality—it’s a reflection of our culture of trust, inclusion, and shared purpose.

Through thoughtful collaboration, the committee directs funding, coordinates volunteer efforts, and strengthens our relationships with organizations doing meaningful work.

Here are just a few of the groups we've been proud to support with volunteer activities, collection drives, event participation, and financial support:

-   [Boys and Girls Club of Truckee Meadows](https://bgctm.org/)
    
-   [Eddy House](https://eddyhouse.org/)
    
-   [The Salvation Army Reno Corps](https://reno.salvationarmy.org/)
    
-   [The Discovery](https://nvdm.org/)
    
-   [Society of Women Engineers Sierra Nevada Section](https://sns.swe.org/)
    
-   [Food Bank of Northern Nevada](https://fbnn.org/)
    

-   Teal Fleur De Lis Foundation
    
-   [Renown Children’s Hospital](https://www.renown.org/)
    
-   [Nevada Recreation & Park Society](https://nevadarecreationampparksociety.wildapricot.org/)
    
-   [The Children’s Cabinet](https://www.childrenscabinet.org/)
    
-   [Reno Rescue Equine Therapy](https://www.renorescue.org/)
    
-   [Junior Achievement of Northern Nevada](https://northernnevada.ja.org/)
    
-   [MATHCOUNTS Northern Nevada Competitions](https://www.mathcounts.org/)

J Resort’s reimagined 4th Street entrance improves arrival, visibility, and connection to downtown Reno. Completed within an active resort environment, the project supports ongoing investment in the Neon Line District and enhances the guest experience along one of the city’s most active hospitality corridors.

[Read More](/portfolio-commercial/j-resort-north-expansion)

# [Renown Mail-Order Pharmacy Tenant Improvement](/portfolio-commercial/renown-mail-order-pharmacy)

This second-floor tenant improvement converted existing space in an operating support-services building into a dedicated mail-order pharmacy environment with offices, staff areas, casework, glazing, MEP upgrades, ceilings, lighting, and finishes. PB coordinated construction around ongoing operations while supporting infrastructure needs for specialized pharmacy automation equipment installed by others.

[Read More](/portfolio-commercial/renown-mail-order-pharmacy)

A vacant behavioral health facility in Reno is being renewed into a public behavioral health center serving Northern Nevada. # NV Legislature Building West Addition & Security Plaza

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)[Featured](/portfolio-commercial/category/Featured)

Jan 30

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Nevada Legislative Counsel Bureau

### Location

Carson City, NV

### Size

13,000-sq-ft

### Designers

Paul Cavin, Architect; Ethos Three Architecture

### Photos

Evan Petty Aerial Imaging

### **A Civic Landmark, Reimagined for the Next Generation**

**Built Around the Legislative Calendar**

Few public buildings in Nevada experience the intensity of activity found on our legislative campus. During session, lawmakers, staff, lobbyists, media, and members of the public all converge in one place, making access, circulation, and security essential to the building's success.

Construction planning centered on those realities. Interior improvements were completed in time to support the legislative session, while the overall project was carefully phased to respect the building's unique role in state government. Today, the completed facility provides a significantly improved experience for everyone who passes through its doors.

**A Better Experience for the Public**

Visitors now enter through a new, three-story, modernized west entrance designed to improve both security and accessibility. An enhanced screening area, an expanded lobby, intuitive wayfinding, and improved reception spaces create a more welcoming arrival while maintaining the level of security expected in a civic building.

New conference and briefing rooms provide much-needed flexibility for legislative business, while ADA-compliant restroom upgrades and a deli improve comfort and convenience for lawmakers, staff, and visitors during long days on campus.

**Building Within an Active Government Campus**

Success depended on careful coordination as much as construction expertise. Working within an occupied government complex required detailed planning for deliveries, staging, trade sequencing, and access. Limited space, heightened security, and ongoing public activity demanded constant communication between the project team, campus stakeholders, and design professionals.

**A Lasting Civic Presence**

The exterior renovation of the west face of the building replaces aging cladding with granite quarried from the Sierra Nevada range, creating a façade designed for generations of service. Along with courtyard and plaza improvements, the new western exterior presents a timeless architectural character that complements neighboring civic buildings while improving durability and long-term performance.

**Built to Serve Nevada**

From security and accessibility to meeting space and public amenities, the completed project strengthens how Nevada's legislative process functions while creating a more welcoming environment for everyone who comes to participate in state government. Plenium Builders is proud to have been a part of this historic transformation.

# Eric & Linda Lannes Basketball Building - University of Nevada, Reno

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)[Featured](/portfolio-commercial/category/Featured)

Aug 12

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

University of Nevada, Reno

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

12,000-sq-ft

### Designer

HOK Architects

### Photos

In1ViewMedia

The largest single donation in UNR history, Eric (‘82) and Linda Lannes gave $4,000,000 to jump start the design and construction of new locker rooms and lounges for the men’s and women’s basketball teams next to the Ramon Sessions Basketball Performance Center. Sessions, another Plenium Builders project completed in 2017, converted the existing Lombardi building into UNR’s first dedicated indoor practice courts and a state-of-the-art strength training facility.

These days, part of attracting and retaining top talent in college sports is providing amenities that enhance the player experience and support the athletes in their desires to become the best on earth at what they do on behalf of their school. UNR hopes to continue to pull in star student athletes by giving them a player-only space to relax, recalibrate, and prepare mentally for the games ahead.

Designed by San Francisco-based architect HOK, the locker rooms and lounge building project adds nearly 12,000 square feet and two stories of locker rooms, shower rooms, lounge spaces with kitchenettes and pantry, restrooms, flex spaces, and outdoor terraces.

Plenium Builders was awarded the project via the CMAR method of delivery based on team interviews and our fee proposal. The preconstruction team worked alongside the client and design team to shepherd the project from concept to construction, keeping to the approved budget while examining building structure, material cost, and schedule options. Over the 14-month schedule, the construction team kept the project within budget, delivering it on-time for the grand opening.

# Airway Commerce Center

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)[Featured](/portfolio-commercial/category/Featured)

Aug 3

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### **Building Precision**

### Client

Tolles Development

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

890,000-sq-ft

### Designer

Tectonics Design Group

### Photos

Jeff Dow Photography & Motion  
Evan Petty Aerial Imaging

### Awards

NAIOP CRE Industrial Project of the Year  
Nevada AGC PINNACLE Award

Four buildings. A total of over 893,000 square feet. At first glance, the Airway Commerce Center seems like a straightforward industrial project. But beneath its clean lines and efficient design lies an intricate web of coordination and problem-solving—a hallmark of Plenium Builders (PB) and its partnership with Tolles Development. Located strategically near the Reno-Tahoe International Airport, this development demanded not just precision construction but seamless compliance with complex regulations.

**Bringing a Vision to Life**

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/71030260-e257-4804-9f1d-eaca7ae8c33f/airway+commerce+center+4+reno+nv+interior_.jpg)

Tolles Development envisioned an adaptable industrial park designed to accommodate Northern Nevada’s diverse business needs. With PB’s expertise in tilt-up construction, this vision took shape. The team collaborated with stakeholders to meet FAA and Reno-Tahoe Airport standards, ensuring all elements complied with special use permits and environmental considerations.

**Excellence in Tilt-Up Construction**

PB brought their technical skill to tilt-up construction—a process where panels are poured and cured onsite, then lifted into place. This efficient method, widely used in the region, required thousands of precisely placed embedded components to support future structural connections, crane pick points, and utilities. Spanning 52.71 acres, the Airway Commerce Center features 28 grade-level dock doors, 145 dock-high doors, and a 60-foot truck court depth, reflecting the extensive coordination required to create adaptable and functional spaces. The result: a sturdy, adaptable framework that caters to various tenants, from office spaces to distribution centers.

**Overcoming Challenges**

![](https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/6234ed17ed89810e30ea47f5/0433db1d-e697-4f19-9aae-279393ab339f/airway+commerce+reno+nevada+19_.jpg)

From relocating a FEMA floodway to designing within a trapezoidal runway easement, Tolles Development and design team leader Tectonics Design Group tackled some of the project’s most complex engineering challenges. The floodway relocation involved extensive engineering to redirect water flow without compromising surrounding infrastructure, ensuring compliance with environmental and safety regulations. The trapezoidal runway easement demanded precise design adjustments to align with FAA guidelines, preserving airfield operations while optimizing site usability. Their innovative approach ensured compliance with FAA regulations, addressed critical infrastructure needs, and balanced stringent timelines. These achievements, supported by seamless coordination among multiple agencies, demonstrate their ability to navigate regulatory demands while maintaining operational efficiency.

**A Landmark for Sustainability**

Sustainable landscaping with low-water vegetation, including 440 trees and 1,500 shrubs and perennials, enhances the site’s natural aesthetic while conserving water and adhering to FAA guidelines for airfield compatibility. Separately, high-efficiency mechanical systems provide energy savings and operational reliability, contributing to long-term environmental benefits. Additionally, the site’s location and proximity to transportation and shipping hubs help lower carbon emissions for tenants by reducing travel distances, depending on their business needs. These features reflect a commitment to environmentally conscious development.

**Setting a New Standard**

The Airway Commerce Center exemplifies PB’s ability to transform a complex project into a seamless reality. This development is a model for industrial innovation and collaborative success, setting a new benchmark for precision and sustainability in Northern Nevada.

# New Deantronics Nevada Manufacturing and R&D Facility

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)[Featured](/portfolio-commercial/category/Featured)

Aug 1

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

New Deantronics

### Location

Sparks, NV

### Size

200,000-sq-ft

### Designer

Tectonics Design Group

New Deantronics chose the Reno-Tahoe community for their U.S. manufacturing, research, and innovation hub—part of their initiative to create a global presence linked to the firm’s two Taiwan facilities and their established US operations. The first of multiple planned structures on the 14-acre campus, this building includes office space, warehouse, laboratories, a meditation room, and a cafeteria with an enclosed courtyard.

Inside the 100,000-square-foot, high-bay warehouse is a prefabricated, 25,000-square-foot, Class 100,000 (ISO8) cleanroom suite. Sweeping curves and a mix of wood, metals, stone, and glass bring together the natural palette, reflective of the Feng Shui principles embedded in the design and construction process. The main entrance, which houses a 180-square-foot living “green wall”, also contains a wealth vase, buried in the foundation to attract prosperity and longevity for the firm. Constructed during a global pandemic, material procurement was a substantial challenge which the team mitigated with alternates and overtime for critical-path trades.

# Wolf Pack Fieldhouse - University of Nevada, Reno

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Aug 4

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

**A New Era for Campus Wellness and Athletics**

### Client

Tolles Development

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

72,000-sq-ft

### Designer

Tectonics Design Group

### Photos

Evan Petty, Aerial Imaging

Step inside the next chapter of the University of Nevada, Reno’s campus experience, where students, athletes, and the community will soon gather under one expansive roof. The new indoor fieldhouse, now under construction adjacent to Mackay Stadium, is set to become a hub for sports, student life, and wellness. From the earliest stages of planning, the project has been defined by collaboration—between Tolles Development as the project lead, the University’s dedicated staff, and a design team committed to creating a facility that meets a wide range of campus needs.

**Preconstruction Partnership in Action**

Early involvement in preconstruction allowed the entire team to map out the project’s complexities and design an implementation plan that prioritized value and minimized disruption to daily campus operations. The open exchange of ideas with University representatives and the design team led to thoughtful solutions for everything from student safety to event scheduling. This careful approach has set the stage for construction to proceed on budget, with risk mitigated at every step.

**A Facility Designed for All**

At 72,000 square feet, the fieldhouse offers 110 yards of indoor turf—ample space for Nevada club and intramural sports, year-round practice for Wolf Pack Athletics, and a host of campus and community activities. Flexible by design, the facility accommodates training for football, soccer, and softball, along with marching band rehearsals, ROTC drills, student events, and youth programs. The vision: a welcoming, multi-purpose venue where wellness and connection are front and center.

**Built on Long-Term Relationships**

Few projects come together as smoothly as those rooted in lasting partnerships. Some team members have been working on the UNR campus for nearly three decades, and that experience brings a depth of insight to each phase of the project. Tolles Development’s leadership and the University’s proactive involvement have ensured that challenges are anticipated and resolved quickly. The shared goal: to deliver a state-of-the-art fieldhouse that enhances the student experience for generations to come.

# Renown NW Reno Urgent Care Clinic

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Sep 25

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Renown Health

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

5,900-sq-ft

### Designer

OneStudio D+A

### Awards

NAIOP CRE Award

### Photos

Evan Petty Aerial Imaging

**From Paint Store to Patient Clinic**

What began as a simple commercial box, previously a paint supply store, has been transformed into a fully functional urgent care clinic. Renown Health sought to expand services in Northwest Reno, using the available building footprint to meet healthcare delivery needs quickly and cost-effectively.

The interior was reimagined to support both patient care and back-of-house operations, with attention to flow, flexibility, and system performance.

**Designed for Efficiency**

Seven private exam rooms, a clinical lab, open and enclosed staff work areas, and a welcoming reception area anchor the layout. New mechanical, electrical, and plumbing systems were installed to replace outdated infrastructure and support the demands of clinical use. Advanced medical communication systems were also integrated to streamline care delivery and enable real-time coordination between staff. These upgrades turn a once-generic space into a fast-moving clinical environment built around quality care.

**Phased Delivery for Real-World Timelines**

Long-lead electrical components, particularly switchgear tied to X-ray activation, threatened to delay the entire project. To maintain the opening date, the team phased construction to allow occupancy and use of the facility while the final gear is procured and installed. The X-ray suite is fully built out and will be activated in a subsequent phase. In the meantime, the remainder of the clinic is getting ready to open to serve the community.

**A Shell Transformed**

Working within the constraints of an existing retail structure required precise layout coordination and creative system routing. Field crews adapted design details on-site to accommodate unforeseen conditions, aligning health code requirements with architectural limitations. The end result is clean, functional, and responsive to Renown’s operational goals.

## WEBSITE PRIVACY POLICY

Plenium Builders respects your privacy. Plenium Builders’ Privacy Policy describes the collection, storage, use, and sharing of information obtained on, through or by our website, pleniumbuilders.com. Please read this Privacy Policy. Your access or use of pleniumbuilders.com indicates your consent and agreement with the terms of this Privacy Policy. Please do not access or use pleniumbuilders.com if you disagree with any part of this Privacy Policy. If you have questions about this Privacy Policy please contact us at info@pleniumbuilders.com or 775-398-0123.

### Special notice regarding links to other websites

Links to websites that are not owned or operated by or for Plenium Builders (“third-party websites”) may be provided on pleniumbuilders.com and are provided solely as a convenience. If you access such links you will necessarily leave pleniumbuilders.com and this Privacy Policy will not apply to the third-party websites. Examples of links to third party websites found on pleniumbuilders.com include, but are not limited to, news organizations, event registrations, government resources, philanthropic donation forms, etc.

### Personal and Other Information

For purpose of this Privacy Policy, we may obtain “Personal Information” and/or “Other Information.” Personal Information means any information that can be used to identify you as an individual, which may include, but may not be limited to, first name, last name, title, email address(es), postal address(es), and telephone number(s). We also may obtain “Other Information” that does not alone identify you as an individual, which may include, but may not be limited to, internet protocol (IP) address, browser type and version, devices you use to access pleniumbuilders.com, and information about how you use and navigate our website.

### Collection of Information

We collect Personal Information when you provide it to us. For example, on certain pages on pleniumbuilders.com we may provide online forms in which you can input information including your name and email address to request information, subscribe to our newsletters, submit an employment application, contact us for a general inquiry, or for other purposes indicated on pleniumbuilders.com.

We collect Other Information when you access and use pleniumbuilders.com. For example, our website may automatically gather certain information and store it in log files when you use our website, including IP addresses and/or click-stream data. An IP address is a number that is automatically assigned to your computer when you access the Internet. When you request web pages from our website your IP addresses is logged. We may also collect click-stream data when you request specific pages from our website. Click-stream data may include such information as the page served, time, source of the request, type of browser making the request, preceding page view, and other such non-personal information.

We collect information through Cookies when you access and use pleniumbuilders.com. Cookies are technologies widely used for security purposes, to learn about and improve how users navigate a website, and to enable features or functions of a website. Cookies are small often encrypted text or data files that are transferred to your device or hard drive when your browser settings permit the acceptance of cookies. The request for these files from a server reveals certain information, such as your browser type and IP address, and cookies are created when a user’s browser loads a particular website. The website sends information to the browser which then creates a text file. Every time the user returns to the same website the browser retrieves and sends this text file to the website’s server. To learn more about cookies and how to turn them on or off on your browser’s settings menu please visit allaboutcookies.org.

### Use of Collected Information

#### Personal Information

We do not sell Personal Information.

We use Personal Information collected from you as necessary to provide the information or services you have requested, such as to respond to your inquiry, process your subscription to one of our newsletters, register you for a Plenium-Builders-hosted event, or mail to you a printed Plenium Builders publication, among other appropriate purposes.

Unless you have unsubscribed or otherwise notified us of your desire not to receive such information, when appropriate our Marketing Department may use your Personal Information to inform you about developments at Plenium Builders; our preconstruction services; recent team accomplishments; our networking, business development and community events, webinars and activities; updates regarding construction technology; and information relevant to the client industries we serve.

#### Other Information

We do not sell Other Information.

We use Other Information for purposes of website system administration and to understand how visitors use our website. Tracking technologies and other website usage data help us determine how visitors arrive at our website, what content is most popular, which features are most important, what types of visitors in the aggregate are interested in particular kinds of information, and similar analytical information. We use this information to update our website design and organization, improve navigation and content, and provide additional experiential and navigational technologies to ensure we are meeting the needs of our website visitors. We may also use Other Information as part of general marketing research.

You can opt out of receiving marketing-oriented communications at any time by clicking the unsubscribe link in the associated email communication or by sending an email to info@pleniumbuilders.com.

#### Sharing of Information

Certain services on our website are provided by third parties and we may use various third parties to help in whole or in part to host, operate, analyze, maintain, upgrade, monitor, improve, and/or secure our website. We may share certain limited Personal Information and/or Other Information with such entities only as specifically needed for them to perform the particular task(s) described in Services Agreements signed with such entities.

We may share certain limited Personal Information and/or Other Information with third party communications or marketing services providers that assist us with communicating or marketing to users of our website or individuals that request information from us through our website. For example, we may share certain limited information, such as your email address, with a third-party systems provider that distributes to you email notifications on behalf of Plenium Builders about our events and announcements, among others. Similarly, we may share certain limited information with third-party organizations with whom we may collaborate on marketing-oriented activities or events and use of such information will be limited to the specific activity or event.

We do not sell any information collected on, through or by our website nor do we provide Personal Information or Other Information to third parties for purposes of them marketing their products or services to you.

#### Protection of Personal Information

Plenium Builders uses industry standard security measures to protect Personal Information collected on, through or by pleniumbuilders.com. We implement numerous physical, procedural, organizational, administrative, and technological safeguards that are designed to keep your information private and secure. These measures are adapted to the nature of the information in our custody. We also take measures to: (1) protect the ongoing confidentiality, integrity, availability, and resilience of our processing systems and services; (2) restore the availability of and access to Personal Information in a timely manner in the event of a physical or technical incident; and (3) test, assess and evaluate our measures. However, even though Plenium Builders makes reasonable efforts to protect information in its custody, no security system or data storage or transmission over the Internet or Cloud or otherwise is 100% secure. Thus, we cannot guarantee the 100% privacy or security of any information we may have collected or received from you on, through or by pleniumbuilders.com. If you have reason to believe your information is not private or secure, please immediately notify us at info@pleniumbuilders.com.

#### Changes to this Privacy Policy

Your continued use of pleniumbuilders.com means you consent to and agree with the terms of any new, changed or updated Privacy Policy. If you have questions about this Privacy Policy, please email us at info@pleniumbuilders.com.

## Copyright and Image Use Policy

All content on this website, including but not limited to photographs, images, text, graphics, and other materials, is protected by copyright and other intellectual property laws. Unauthorized reproduction, distribution, or use of any materials from this website is strictly prohibited.

Many of the images featured on this website are licensed from third-party professional photographers. These images are used by Plenium Builders under **rights-managed agreements** that limit where and how the images can be displayed. We do not own these photographs outright and **cannot grant permission** for others to use them.

If you are interested in using any content from this website, including photographs or project descriptions, you may inquire via email at info@pleniumbuilders.com.

**Plenium Builders takes the** **rights of photographers and content creators very seriously**. Use of Plenium Builders’ website content in violation of our agreements may result in legal action.

# J Resort North Expansion

Jul 1

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Jacobs Entertainment

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

12,085-sq-ft

### Designer

TBE Architects

### Photos

Evan Petty Aerial Imaging

**A New Front Door for a Downtown Destination**

Along West 4th Street, a reimagined resort entrance gives guests a clearer, more memorable welcome to one of downtown Reno’s most visible hospitality and entertainment properties. The work supports Jacobs Entertainment’s broader vision for J Resort and Reno’s Neon Line District, where entertainment, dining, public art, and community activity continue to reshape the western edge of downtown.

_Watch the project happen in just 30 seconds!_

**A Guest Experience Rooted in Place**

The expanded entrance does more than update a façade. It improves wayfinding, strengthens the connection between the resort and the street, and creates a more polished sense of arrival for guests, visitors, and locals. For a property built around movement and activity, the entry experience needed to feel intuitive, durable, and connected to the surrounding district.

**Built Around Active Hospitality Operations**

Construction within an operating resort required careful coordination around access, safety, staging, deliveries, and ongoing property needs. Plenium Builders managed the work to maintain progress while allowing the resort to continue serving guests throughout construction.

Exterior improvements, arrival pathways, and transitions all influence how people experience the property before reaching hotel amenities, restaurants, gaming, or event spaces. Attention to sequencing and site conditions helped preserve the sense of welcome the finished entrance was designed to create.

**Supporting Momentum in Reno’s Neon Line District**

The completed expansion strengthens J Resort’s connection to West 4th Street and supports continued investment in downtown Reno. As part of the broader Neon Line District, the project contributes to a growing destination where people can gather, stay, and explore.

# Renown Mail-Order Pharmacy Tenant Improvement

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Jun 9

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

5,000-sq-ft

### Delivery

Design-Bid-Build

### Designer

OneStudio D+A

### Photos

Evan Petty, Aerial Imaging

**A Practical Pharmacy TI Supporting Better Access**

Better access to prescriptions depends on the spaces that make daily pharmacy work easier, safer, and more efficient. In Reno, a tenant improvement inside an operating support-services building created a dedicated mail-order pharmacy environment designed to help serve patients throughout the region.

The project converted existing second-floor space above Renown’s print shop into a functional healthcare support area with pharmacy operations, offices, an employee lounge, restroom, storage, casework, glazing, updated MEP systems, ceilings, lighting, and finishes. The work required selective demolition, framing, casework, glazing, and finish installation within an active building where nearby operations continued throughout construction.

**Building Within an Operating Facility**

Careful coordination guided the work from start to finish. Access, deliveries, noise, dust control, and trade sequencing all needed to respect the occupied setting while keeping the tenant improvement moving forward. That balance helped maintain a productive jobsite without losing sight of the client’s day-to-day operational needs.

OneStudio D+A developed a design that supported both the technical requirements of the pharmacy and a clean, professional workplace for staff. PB worked with the design team and trade partners to coordinate the buildout, including new systems, lighting, casework, ceilings, and interior finishes.

**Coordinating the Details**

Specialized pharmacy automation equipment, installed by others, added coordination points for surrounding infrastructure, clearances, workflow, and finishes. The pharmacy includes a vial-filling robot capable of filling up to 80 prescriptions per hour, according to information shared by Renown during the opening celebration. Representatives also noted that the technology helps process prescriptions safely and efficiently, with accuracy reported at more than 99.9 percent.

The finished TI provides a bright, organized mail-order pharmacy space that supports staff workflow, prescription delivery, and improved medication access for patients across the region.

# Washoe County Behavioral Health Center

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Jan 19

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Washoe County

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

47,700-sq-ft

### Designer

H+K Architects

### Restoring a Critical Resource

A long-vacant healthcare facility along East Ninth Street is being reimagined to meet one of Northern Nevada’s most urgent needs. Once home to a private behavioral health hospital, the former West Hills property is now undergoing a comprehensive transformation into a publicly accessible behavioral health center that expands care options for youth and families across the region. By reusing an existing structure, Washoe County is accelerating delivery while reducing environmental impact and making efficient use of public investment.

### Early Insight Through CM-at-Risk Collaboration

Engagement began early under a CM-at-Risk delivery model, allowing the construction team to work alongside Washoe County and H+K Architects to evaluate how the building could be adapted for modern behavioral health services. Having sat empty for years, the facility required careful verification before design decisions could move forward. Advanced 3D BIM scanning, performed by Plenium Builders’ in-house virtual construction management team, captured the interior layout and existing systems in detail, providing a reliable digital baseline for coordination, estimating, and phased planning. That early clarity helped the team confirm reuse opportunities while identifying systems that required full replacement.

### Rebuilding Systems for Modern Care

Construction now underway focuses on a comprehensive renewal of building systems and infrastructure. Mechanical and electrical systems are being fully modernized to support current healthcare standards, alongside roof and building envelope replacement. Life safety, communications, and security upgrades are integrated throughout the facility, creating a safe, resilient environment for both patients and staff. Within the existing footprint, interior spaces are being reconfigured to support inpatient behavioral health programs, clinical and group therapy areas, educational classrooms, and staff support functions, all designed to promote dignity, healing, and flexibility.

### Executing Work in an Urban Setting

Located near a busy corridor and community event venues, the site required thoughtful logistics planning despite the absence of active operations within the building itself. Selective demolition, controlled sequencing, and early procurement of long-lead components were planned during preconstruction and are now being executed to maintain schedule alignment. State and federal funding adds an additional layer of accountability, addressed through disciplined cost tracking, transparent reporting, and early trade partner involvement.

### Collaboration That Carries Through Construction

As the project advances, collaboration remains central to delivery. The client, architect, and construction team continue to use shared digital models to coordinate systems and resolve field conditions efficiently. That ongoing alignment ensures that every upgrade, whether structural, mechanical, or architectural, supports the broader mission of the facility.

More than a renovation, this project represents a reinvestment in community capacity. By restoring and modernizing an existing healthcare building, Washoe County is expanding access to behavioral health services while creating a center that supports treatment, education, and workforce development. The result will be a facility positioned to serve Northern Nevada with care, stability, and purpose for years to come.

# Virginia Palmer Elementary School Modernization

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Jan 19

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Washoe County School District

### Location

Sun Valley, NV

### Size

13,000-sq-ft (addition)

### Designer

Architects +

### Reimagining an Elementary Campus

A long-term vision for student learning guides the modernization of this elementary school in Sun Valley. Identified in the District’s Facility Modernization Plan as a pilot for a “pod-style” elementary school prototype, the project will organize classrooms into connected learning communities supported by shared spaces, while expanding permanent learning areas, improving safety, and refreshing existing facilities.

### A CM-at-Risk Partnership in Preconstruction

Using a CM-at-Risk delivery method, the team is supporting Washoe County School District throughout design by evaluating constructability, costs, and sequencing as the work takes shape. Preconstruction services focus on translating educational goals into practical, buildable solutions that will align with district standards and the available budget. Ongoing coordination with Architects + helps design intent and site logistics evolve together.

### Creating Permanent Space for Learning

The planned expansion will replace existing temporary facilities with permanent, flexible environments that support collaborative learning and evolving instructional approaches. New spaces will include a student hub, media center, STEAM-focused learning areas, shaded outdoor classrooms, and general-use restrooms. Interior renovations and refreshes will improve functionality for both instructional programs and administrative staff.

### Safety and Campus Organization

Safety improvements shape early planning decisions. The project will strengthen campus security through a new single point of entry, updated surveillance, and enhanced fencing. Preconstruction efforts evaluate how these improvements will integrate into the existing campus layout while supporting daily school operations.

### Phased Planning for a Working School

Construction will occur in phases while the campus remains operational. Preconstruction planning addresses circulation, access, and separation of students from construction activity so instruction can continue without relocation. Careful sequencing helps maintain a safe, functional environment throughout modernization.

### Cost, Schedule, and Decision Support

In the CMAR method of project edelivery, clear cost and schedule information supports informed decision-making as design progresses. Detailed estimates are prepared at schematic design, design development, and construction document phases and keep scope aligned with the project budget. Schedule development accounts for academic calendars, testing periods, and seasonal considerations.

### Preparing for What Comes Next

As design advances, preconstruction lays the groundwork for construction through subcontractor outreach, procurement planning, and GMP development. The result is a well-defined path forward for a campus that will support students now and serve as a model for future elementary school modernizations. Watch the WCSD video here!

# Echo Loder Elementary School Replacement

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Jan 16

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Washoe County School District

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

107,000-sq-ft

### Designer

H+K Architects

### A New Chapter for a Familiar Campus

This long-established elementary school site in Reno is preparing for a major transformation. Identified through the District’s Facility Modernization Plan (FMP), the campus replacement reflects a commitment to updating aging infrastructure while preserving the neighborhood connections that have defined the school for generations.

### Rebuilding Without Leaving the Site

The project will replace the existing school with a new two-story elementary facility constructed on the current campus. A phased approach allows the new building to be completed first, with the original school remaining in place until students and staff can transition into the new space. Once the new facility is occupied, the original structure will be demolished, clearing the way for a new playground and outdoor learning areas. This sequencing minimizes disruption, avoids extended relocations, and provides a clear path from old to new while maintaining daily school operations.

### Designed for Modern Learning

The new building will follow the District’s current elementary prototype, developed to support contemporary teaching models and flexible instruction. PB team members have built three similar elementary prototype schools alongside WCSD and H+K Architects—Poulakidas, Inskeep, and Mitchell.

Planned spaces for the new Loder Elementary School include classrooms designed for adaptability, collaborative group learning areas, and a media center that supports both individual and shared learning experiences.

### Planning for Safety and Logistics

Design-bid-build delivery places added importance on early clarity around access, boundaries, and site logistics. Planning efforts focus on maintaining safe separation between construction activity and occupied school areas, with careful consideration given to circulation, drop-off and pick-up patterns, utilities, and emergency access. Clear communication and defined sequencing will help ensure that the campus remains safe, legible, and functional as the work progresses.

### A Phased Path to Completion

Following completion of the new school, demolition of the original building will open space for a new playground and outdoor amenities. This final phase will complete the campus transformation.

### Supporting District Priorities

As one of the early projects approved under the FMP, the campus replacement represents a long-term investment by WCSD. The District’s approach balances modernization with continuity, delivering a facility built to current educational standards while respecting the site’s role within its surrounding neighborhood.

### Take A Virtual Tour

Courtesy of designer H+K Architects, here is a fly-through of the new school design.

### Gallery

Below is a gallery of design renderings showing the various spaces in the new school.

# TMFPD Station 37

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Jul 22

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Washoe County

### Location

Reno, NV

### Size

4,325-sq-ft

### Designer

TSK Architects

### Photos

Evan Petty

**Community-Focused Expansion in Hidden Valley**  
Tucked into the foothills east of Reno, the expansion of Station 37 brings meaningful upgrades to a vital public safety facility serving the Hidden Valley neighborhood. The project enhances the site’s functionality while respecting the surrounding residential character, reinforcing Truckee Meadows Fire & Rescue’s long-term commitment to the area.

**Upgraded Operations, Unified Design**  
The updated station replaces a cramped and outdated configuration with a modern, purpose-built apparatus bay sized for a structural engine, a brush engine, and an ATV rescue/firefighting vehicle. It connects directly to a converted single-family residence that now houses the station’s offices, kitchen, and sleeping quarters—bringing all essential operations together under one cohesive layout. The design maintains a residential scale, using complementary exterior finishes and low-profile forms to blend with the existing neighborhood.

**Prioritizing Resilience and Service Continuity**  
Construction is underway while Station 37 remains fully staffed and operational, a key requirement that shaped both the phasing strategy and site logistics. The project team worked closely with Truckee Meadows Fire & Rescue to maintain clear access for 24/7 emergency response and to minimize disruptions to nearby residents. A temporary reduction in on-site parking required careful coordination to accommodate firefighter vehicles while keeping neighborhood impacts manageable.

**Support Infrastructure for Emergency Readiness**  
In addition to the primary structure, the project includes new concrete pads for a backup generator and a trash and fuel storage enclosure. A replacement water main is also part of the scope, providing irrigation to adjacent properties and ensuring long-term utility reliability. Paved and landscaped parking for both staff and visitors has been added to the west side of the site, with thoughtful grading and a landscaped berm along Hidden Valley Drive to maintain privacy and visual continuity.

**Collaborative Planning and Neighborhood Sensitivity**  
Every element of the expansion was planned with community access and safety in mind. The public-facing side of the facility includes a welcoming entry and ADA-compliant improvements, while public notices, site signage, and local outreach helped set expectations early in the process. Design choices, such as matching rooflines and scaled-down materials, reflect the project’s intent to serve the neighborhood while fitting into it.

# Carson Valley Swim Center Eastside Expansion

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

May 29

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

East Fork Swimming Pool District

### Location

Minden, NV

### Size

2,500-sq-ft

### Designer

TSK Architects

### Awards

Nevada AGC Pinnacle Award

### Photos

Evan Petty Photography

### A Vital Community Hub

Morning swim lessons, competitive training, and year-round recreation make the Carson Valley Swim Center a vibrant community resource and a place residents genuinely value. As participation grew, the existing entry, storage, and maintenance areas no longer supported operational demands. The challenge was clear: expand and modernize the facility without interrupting daily programming, compromising safety, or disrupting public access.

### Strategic Structural Integration

A 2,500-square-foot east-side addition was carefully tied into the existing structure. Structural and building envelope connections required thoughtful sequencing to protect the integrity of the active natatorium while maintaining weather protection and life-safety systems. Mechanical, pool, and emergency power systems were coordinated and integrated without downtime, preserving operations from start to finish.

### Phased Construction in an Active Environment

Because the Swim Center remained fully operational, construction progressed in deliberate phases. Access points were reconfigured, pedestrian flow was redirected, and safety controls were heightened to protect patrons of all ages. During winter construction, a temporary enclosed, well-lit tunnel provided safe, protected access to an alternate entrance, ensuring comfort and continuity despite seasonal weather challenges.

### Performance, Durability, and Resilience

Energy performance and long-term durability guided the solution. Repositioned entrance doors stabilized interior temperatures and reduced HVAC strain. New concrete flooring with integrated drains improved sanitation and slip resistance. A two-car maintenance garage and climate-controlled workshop now protect district assets and extend equipment lifespan. A permanently installed generator strengthens facility resiliency, enabling continued operation during power outages and supporting County emergency functions when needed.

### Coordinated Delivery

Structural tie-ins, live-environment sequencing, and system integration required disciplined coordination and proactive communication. Director Shannon Harris noted, “The Plenium project team navigated notable design and engineering challenges while maintaining a clear focus on functionality, safety, and long-term performance. Their ability to keep our facility fully operational throughout construction while delivering high-quality results reflects exceptional professionalism.”

The Eastside Expansion enhances efficiency, accessibility, and community resilience while allowing the Swim Center to continue serving the public without missing a day of programming.

# TMFPD Station 35

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Apr 28

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Washoe County

### Location

Sparks, NV

### Size

13,000-sq-ft

### Designer

H+K Architects

### Expanding Emergency Response in a Growing Region

Truckee Meadows Fire Protection District is expanding emergency services with the construction of Fire Station 35, a new facility located off Interstate 80 and Waltham Way. Built to support the growing eastern communities of Washoe County, the station will enhance response capabilities across the I-80 corridor and beyond, helping protect residents, travelers, and future development areas within the Fire District.

### Strategic Coverage for Communities and Corridors

Fire Station 35 will reduce response times from Vista to Wadsworth and ease the load on Station 37 and neighboring agencies including Sparks Fire and Storey County Fire. The station will also play an important role in supporting regional development along USA Parkway and delivering faster mutual aid to Fernley and Wadsworth.

### A Partnership That Supports Public Safety

The new facility was made possible through a major contribution from Apple, which provided land and construction funding. Apple’s investment complements its long-standing operations in the region, including a data center and a satellite ground station that supports Emergency SOS via satellite technology.

### Collaboration to Deliver the Right Facility

Plenium Builders, serving as the Construction Manager at Risk, completed preconstruction efforts on schedule and on budget, collaborating closely with H+K Architects, Truckee Meadows Fire Protection District, and project stakeholders to align the design and scope with the district’s priorities. This early partnership approach helped maintain budget goals while preserving the operational needs of the station.

### A Station Designed for Today and Tomorrow

The 13,000-square-foot facility is designed for round-the-clock staffing, with accommodations for four to eight firefighters per shift. It includes three apparatus bays, a Type 1 Fire Engine, a Type 3 Brush Engine, and a water tender. Space has been planned for future specialty vehicles and a paramedic ambulance as the region’s needs grow.

### Building for the Future of Public Safety

Station 35 positions Truckee Meadows Fire Protection District to meet evolving demands along one of the region’s busiest transportation corridors. It also supports long-term public safety improvements aligned with the Nevada Department of Transportation’s I-80 widening and enhancement project. As construction moves forward, the project team remains committed to delivering a facility that will serve the community for decades to come.

# Edward C. Reed High School Modernization

[Commercial](/portfolio-commercial/category/Commercial)

Apr 23

Written By [Lisa Frisch](/portfolio-commercial?author=52715e4be4b0f3e367a30449)

### Client

Washoe County School District

### Location

Sparks, NV

### Size

160,000-sq-ft

### Designer

Collaborative Design Studio

**An Ambitious Upgrade for a Legacy Campus**

The halls of Edward C. Reed High School in Sparks are preparing for a transformation that goes far beyond a fresh coat of paint. Built in the 1970s, the campus has served generations of students—but now it’s being reimagined to meet the needs of modern education and support today’s learners, staff, and programs.

**A Strategic CM-at-Risk Partnership**

The Washoe County School District selected Plenium Builders as the CM-at-Risk partner to guide this ambitious project from early planning through construction. The effort includes 70,000 square feet of new construction and the renovation of approximately 90,000 square feet within the 237,000-square-foot school. Importantly, this modernization is being completed while the school remains open, requiring careful phasing, proactive coordination, and a strong emphasis on safety.

**A Design Focused on Safety, Flexibility, and Function**

Working alongside Collaborative Design Studio, the team has developed a campus-wide upgrade strategy that prioritizes student safety, learning environments, and functionality. The expanded footprint will introduce new facilities for athletics, band, classrooms, and a student hub. Portable buildings will be removed entirely. A renovated administration wing with a secure single-point entry will anchor the campus, with upgrades throughout the corridors, restrooms, media center, and athletic areas.

**Sitewide Enhancements for a Modern Learning Environment**

Safety improvements are woven throughout the design, including updated fire alarm systems, new access control and security cameras, and robust perimeter fencing. Site enhancements include an overhauled courtyard designed for outdoor learning and gathering, as well as modernized athletic fields and infrastructure upgrades such as new internal waste lines.

**Smart Planning for Ongoing Operations**

With phased construction set to begin in 2025, Plenium Builders is leveraging preconstruction insights to shape a schedule that keeps the school community informed and operations uninterrupted. The team’s approach emphasizes constructability, flexibility, and the long-term maintainability of every decision—ensuring the school district’s investment supports future growth, not just present needs.

**A Campus Reimagined Through Partnership**

From behind-the-scenes coordination to visible improvements that redefine the campus experience, the Reed High School modernization is more than a project. It’s a promise to the community—one built on planning, partnership, and performance.

### Project Renderings

# [NV Legislature Building West Addition & Security Plaza](/portfolio-commercial/nv-legislature-west-add)

The completed modernization improves security, access, meeting capacity, amenities, and exterior durability at one of Nevada’s key civic buildings. Work included a reconfigured west entry, upgraded screening, new meeting spaces, a deli, courtyard upgrades, and a granite façade sourced from the Sierra Nevada range.

# [Eric & Linda Lannes Basketball Building - University of Nevada, Reno](/portfolio-commercial/unr-lannes-basketball-building)

The Eric & Linda Lannes Basketball Building at the University of Nevada, Reno provides state-of-the-art locker rooms, lounge spaces, and amenities for the men’s and women’s basketball programs. The 12,000-square-foot facility enhances recruitment, training, and student-athlete development while strengthening the University’s commitment to athletic excellence.
